ANNUAL REPORT 年 報
ANNUAL REPORT 年報




                   本年度報告封面以「綠色能源驅動可持續未來」為核心概念，通過象徵性
                   的植物生長結構，藝術化呈現昆侖能源多元化的業務佈局及其對人民生
                   活的積極影響。

                   內頁延續封面生態意象，以延伸的枝葉貫穿章節，視覺化呈現昆侖能源
                   業務拓展，強調綠色能源對地球環保的實質貢獻，呼應昆侖能源可持續
                   發展及賦能美好生活的使命。

                   This year’s annual report cover takes “Green Energy Drives a Sustainable
                   Future” as its core concept. Through symbolic plant growth structures,
                   it artistically presents Kunlun Energy’s diversified business layout and its
                   positive impact on people’s lives.

                   The inner pages continue the ecological imagery of the cover, with extended
                   branches and leaves running through the chapters, visually depicting the
                   Company’s business expansion. This highlights the substantial contributions
                   of green energy to environmental protection, aligning with the Company’s
                   mission of sustainable development and empowering a better life.

         intangible assets and exchange gains/losses.                                            虧損之除所得稅費用前溢利。

Notes:                                                                                       附註：

(1)      Net profit margin is profit for the year divided by revenue.                        (1)   淨利潤率指年內溢利除以收入。
(2)      Return on total assets is profit for the year divided by average total assets.      (2)   總資產回報率指年內溢利除以平均資產總額。
(3)      Return on capital employed is the ratio of profit before income tax expense         (3)   淨資本回報率指除所得稅費用及利息前溢利
         and interest divided by total assets less current liabilities.                            除以總資產減流動負債。
(4)      Liabilities-to-Adjusted EBITDA ratio is the ratio of total liabilities divided by   (4)   負債對經調整 EBITDA 倍數指總負債除以經調
         adjusted EBITDA.                                                                          整利息、稅項、折舊、攤銷前盈利。
(5)      Debts-to-net assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings    (5)   淨資產負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除
         and lease liabilities divided by total equity.                                            以總權益。
(6)      Gearing ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings, and lease     (6)   資本負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以
         liabilities divided by the sum of total equity, interest-bearing borrowings and           總權益、計息借貸與租賃負債之和。
         lease liability.
(7)      Debts-to-assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings        (7)   資產負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以
         and lease liabilities divided by total assets.                                            總資產。
(8)      Average finance cost is the ratio of aggregated amount of each category of          (8)   平均融資成本指各種類借貸成本乘其佔總借
         borrowing multiply by its interest rate divided by the total borrowing.                   貸比例，然後加總。
(9)      Dividend payout ratio is dividend per share divided by core basic earnings per      (9)   派息比例指每股股息，除以每股基本核心盈利。
         share.

                                                       昆侖能源有限公司             二零二四年年度報告   11
Chairman’s
Statement
主席報告
                                                                              Chairman’s Statement
                                                                                               主席報告



Respected shareholders:                                                  尊敬的各位股東：
During the previous year, the international pricing of oil and gas and   過去一年，國際油氣價格和供需形勢更加複
the supply-demand dynamic were more complex, characterised by            雜，貿易格局深度調整，「歐降亞增」特徵突
an in-depth adjustment in the trading pattern. The signal of “European   出，亞太消費貢獻全球一半增量。中國經濟

                                                                                                 管理層討論與分析



In 2024, Kunlun Energy adhered to the corporate vision of “Empowering China, Enriching Lives”, continued to improve its governance
system, focused on upgrading the quality of its development, stimulated in-depth value creation, and realised consolidation and
growth of the main business, an increasing growth of emerging businesses, further new highs of operating results, and created a new
landscape for sustainable development. The Company has always adhered to the five development strategies of “Innovation, Green,
Market, Capital and Low Cost”, focused on efficiency and market orientation, implemented precise marketing and created service
value, with the high-end and high-efficiency market continuing to grow, the production and operation situation remaining stable and
positive, and the operations have reached a new level. All major business segments have achieved excellent performance.


2024 年，昆侖能源始終秉持「助力美麗中國建設、點靚人民美好生活」企業願景，持續完善治理體系，聚力提升
發展品質，深入激發價值創造，實現主營業務鞏固壯大、新興業務日益增進、經營業績再創新高，可持續發展
開創新局面。公司始終堅持「創新、綠色、市場、資本、低成本」五大發展戰略，以效益為中心，以市場為導向，
實施精準營銷，創造服務價值，高端高效市場不斷壯大，生產經營形勢平穩向好，經營水平再上台階。各主要
業務板塊均取得了良好業績。

Dividend Policy                                                               股息政策
The dividend policy of the Company allows shareholders to share the           本公司之股息政策允許股東分享本公司的利
Company’s profits and also enables the Company to retain sufficient           潤，同時亦可為本公司的未來發展保留足夠
reserves for its future development. Under the circumstances that             的儲備。在本公司盈利且不影響本集團正常
the Company makes profits and has no effect on the Group’s normal             經營的情況下，本公司擬以年度股息形式與
business operation, the Company intends to share its profits with             股東分享溢利，每年派息總額不少於本公司
its shareholders in the form of annual dividend, which shall be in a          股東應佔本集團合併年度溢利之 25%，惟需
total distribution amount of not less than 25% of the Group’s annual          受以下「考慮因素」所限。
consolidated profit attributable to the shareholders of the Company, on
the condition that it shall be subject to the following “Criteria”.

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS                                       風險管理及內部監控
It is the responsibility of the Board to ensure that the Company            董事會負責確保本公司之內部監控系統穩健
maintains sound and effective internal controls to safeguard the            妥善而且有效，以保障股東之投資及本公司
shareholders’ investment and the Company’s assets. The internal             之資產。本公司之內部監控系統包含完善之
control system of the Company comprises a well-established                  公司架構以及全面之政策及標準。本公司亦
organizational structure and comprehensive policies and standards. The      設有內部審計部門，負責監控本集團內部管
Company also has an internal audit department, which is responsible         治及監督本集團風險管理及內部控制系統。
for monitoring the internal governance of the Group and overseeing the
risk management and internal control system of the Group.

The Board, through the Audit Committee, assesses annually the               董事會透過審核委員會每年評核本集團內部
effectiveness of the Group’s internal control system which covers           監控系統之效能。該系統涵蓋所有重要監控，
all material controls, including financial, operational and compliance      包括本公司的財務、營運及遵守法規的監控
controls as well as risk management of the Company. The Board               以及風險管理。董事會明白，設計有關風險
acknowledges that such risk management and internal control systems         管理及內部控制系統的原意乃管理而非排除
are designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to         未能達成業務目標的風險，並只能就重大錯
achieve business objectives, and can only provide reasonable and            誤陳述或損失提供合理而非絕對的保證。經
not absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss. Having        審閱外部諮詢公司發行的評估報告後，董事
reviewed the assessment report issued by an external advisory firm, the     認為，本集團風險管理及內部控制系統有效
Board considers that the risk management and internal control systems       及充足。
of the Group are effective and adequate.

The board of directors (the “Board”) of Kunlun Energy Company 昆侖能源有限公司（「本公司」）董事會（「董事

the audited consolidated financial statements for the year ended 31 （「本年度」）之年報連同經審核綜合財務報表。
December 2024 (the “Year”).

PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES                                                          主要業務
The Company acts as an investment holding company. The principal              本公司為投資控股公司，其附屬公司、聯營
activities of its subsidiaries, associates and joint ventures are urban gas   公司及合營企業主要業務為城市燃氣配送、
distribution, LNG processing and transportation, LPG sales, integrated        LNG 加工及運輸、LPG 銷售、綜合能源及新

            basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised                計估計之修訂僅影響估計修訂期

              monthly cash flow budget to ensure that the Group                          管理層每月編製現金流量

              obligations as they fall due. The Group arranges and                       夠的流動資金履行到期財

        The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                    本集團管理資本之目標是保障其
        safeguard its ability to continue as a going concern,                  持續經營之能力，以優化股東回
        optimise returns for shareholders and to minimise its cost             報及降低其資本成本。於達至本
        of capital. In meeting its objectives of managing capital, the         集團之資本管理目標方面，本集
        Group may issue new shares, adjust its debt levels or the              團可發行新股份、調整其負債水
        mix between short-term and long-term borrowings.                       平或短期與長期借貸之間的組合。

        The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing                 本 集 團 根 據 資 本 負 債 率（以 計 息
        ratio which is calculated as the sum of interest-bearing               借貸及租賃負債之和除以總權
        borrowings and lease liabilities divided by the sum of total           益、計息借貸及租賃負債之和計
        equity, interest-bearing borrowings and lease liabilities.             算）監察資本。於 2024 年 12 月 31
        The gearing ratio at 31 December 2024 is 21.46% (2023:                 日之資本負債率為 21.46%（2023
        22.71%).                                                               年：22.71%）。

        There were no changes in the management’s approach to                  管理層對本集團的資本管理方針
        capital management of the Group during the year. Neither               在年內並無變更。本公司及其附
        the Company nor any of its subsidiaries are subject to                 屬公司均不受來自外部的資本要
        externally imposed capital requirements.                               求所限。

                  the reporting period on a recurring basis, categorised                  於報告期末按經常性基準
                  into the three-level fair value hierarchy. The level                    計 量，並 分 類 為 公 平 值 計
                  into which a fair value measurement is classified                       量所界定之三級公平值架
                  is determined with reference to the observability                       構。將 公 平 值 計 量 分 類 之
                  and significance of the inputs used in the valuation                    層級乃經參考如下估值方

                         date.                                                                調整報價計量之公平
                                                                                              值。

                         valuation techniques which maximise the use                          值技術計量之公平值，
                         of observable market data and rely as little                         該等估值技術盡量利
                         as possible on entity-specific estimates. If                         用可觀察市場數據而
                         all significant inputs required to fair value an                     極少依賴實體的特定
                         instrument are observable, the instrument is                         估 計。倘 工 具 的 公 平
                         included in level 2.                                                 值所需所有重大輸入
                                                                                              數據均為可觀察數據，
                                                                                              則該工具計入第二級。

                  –      Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using                    －   第 三 級 估 值：使 用 重
                         significant unobservable inputs.                                     大不可觀察輸入數據
                                                                                              計量之公平值。

4     CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND                                   4   關鍵會計判斷及估計
      ESTIMATES
      Accounting judgments and estimates are regularly evaluated                本集團定期根據過往經驗及其他因素
      by the Group, based on historical experience and other factors           （包括對未來事件在當前情況下認為合
      which include expectations of future events that are believed to          理之預期）對會計判斷及估計進行評估。
      be reasonable under the circumstances.

5     REVENUE (CONTINUED)                                                  5   收入（續）
      Accounting policy of revenue recognition:                                收入確認會計政策：

      Income is classified by the Group as revenue when it arises from         當收益產生自本集團日常業務過程中
      the sale of goods, the provision of services or the use by others        之 貨 品 銷 售、提 供 之 服 務 或 他 人 使 用
      of the Group’s assets in the ordinary course of the Group’s              本 集 團 資 產，則 本 集 團 會 將 該 收 益 分
      business.                                                                類為收入。

      Revenue is recognised when control over a product or service             收入在產品或服務之控制權轉讓予客
      is transferred to the customer, or the lessee has the right to use       戶或在承租人有權使用該資產時獲確
      the asset, at the amount of promised consideration to which              認，金 額 為 本 集 團 預 期 有 權 收 取 之 承
      the Group is expected to be entitled, excluding those amounts            諾 代 價，不 包 括 代 表 第 三 方 收 取 之 有
      collected on behalf of third parties. Revenue excludes value             關 金 額。收 入 不 包 括 增 值 稅 或 其 他 銷
      added tax or other sales taxes and is after deduction of any trade       售稅，並扣減任何貿易折扣。
      discounts.

      (i)   Sales of goods                                                     (i)   銷售貨品
            Revenue is recognised when the customer takes                            收入於客戶管有並接納產品時獲
            possession of and accepts the products. If the products are              確認。倘產品屬部分履行涵蓋其
            a partial fulfilment of a contract covering other goods and/             他貨品及╱或服務之合約，則確
            or services, then the amount of revenue recognised is an                 認之收入金額為合約項下總交易
            appropriate proportion of the total transaction price under              價之適當比例，按相對獨立售價
            the contract, allocated between all the goods and services               基準根據合約協定之全部貨品及
            promised under the contract on a relative standalone selling             服務之間分配。
            price basis.

      (ii) Rendering of services                                               (ii) 提供服務
            The Group’s revenue from rendering of services is primarily              本集團提供服務之收入源於提供
            attributable to provision of LNG gasification and unloading              LNG 氣化及卸載服務。提供該等
            services. Revenue from the rendering of these services                   服務之收入於完成 LNG 氣化或卸
            is recognised in the profit or loss upon the gasification or             載後確認。
            unloading of LNG.

      operates.

            (i)       Under the Bermuda Companies Act 1981, the share premium                    (i)     根 據 百 慕 達 1981 年 公 司 法，股

                      amount of the Company’s shares issued for the acquisition.                         之差額。

                      considerations and the aggregate share capital of subsidiaries                     併所收購附屬公司之代價與總股
                      acquired under business combinations under common control.                         本之差額。

                                                                                                         投資公平值之累積淨變動。

                      statements of foreign operations.

33 SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                 33 板塊資料
   Operating segments are reported in a manner consistent with            營運板塊之呈報方式與向主要營運決
   the internal reporting provided to the chief operating decision        策 者 提 供 之 內 部 報 告 貫 徹 一 致，乃 由
   maker which is determined by the Group’s most senior executive         本集團最高管理層及本公司執行董事
   management and Executive Directors of the Company.                     決定。

   The Group organises its business around products and services.         本 集 團 按 產 品 及 服 務 組 織 其 業 務。就
   From the products and services perspective, the Group is               產 品 及 服 務 而 言，本 集 團 廣 泛 從 事 一
   engaged in a broad range of oil and gas related activities and         系 列 石 油 和 天 然 氣 相 關 業 務，其 收 入
   derives its revenue from its four operating segments: Natural          來自四個營運板塊：天然氣銷售、LPG
   Gas Sales, Sales of LPG, LNG Processing and Terminal and               銷售、LNG 加工與儲運及勘探與生產。
   Exploration and Production.

   The Natural Gas Sales segment includes the retail sales,               天然氣銷售板塊包括不同天然氣產
   distribution and trading of various natural gas products. The          品 的 零 售 及 分 銷 與 貿 易。LPG 銷 售 板
   Sales of LPG segment includes the wholesales and retail sales of       塊 包 括 不 同 LPG 產 品 的 批 發 與 零 售。
   various LPG products. LNG Processing and Terminal segment              LNG 加 工 與 儲 運 板 塊 包 括 LNG 加 工、
   includes the processing, unloading, storage, gasification and          卸載、儲存、氣化及裝車。勘探與生產
   entrucking of LNG. The Exploration and Production segment              板塊包括原油及天然氣之勘探、開發、
   includes the exploration, development, production and sales of         生產和銷售。
   crude oil and natural gas.

   The senior executive management of the Group and the                   本集團管理層及執行董事根據各板塊
   Executive Directors assess the performance of the operating            之 除 所 得 稅 費 用、應 佔 聯 營 公 司 及 合
   segments based on each segment’s profit before income tax              資企業之溢利減虧損前之溢利評估經
   expense, and share of profits less losses of associates and joint      營板塊之表現（「板塊業績」）。
   ventures (“segment results”).

   Segment assets exclude deferred and current taxes, other               板 塊 資 產 不 包 括 遞 延 及 即 期 稅 項、其
   financial assets, investments in associates and joint ventures.        他 金 融 資 產、於 聯 營 公 司 及 合 資 企 業
                                                                          之投資。

   Corporate results, net, mainly refers to interest income earned        公司收支淨額主要指現金及現金等值
   from cash and cash equivalents, net exchange gains/(losses),           項目所賺取的利息收入、匯兌收益╱（虧
   general and administrative expenses and interest expenses              損）淨額、公司層面產生之一般性和管
   incurred at corporate level.                                           理費用及利息支出。

   Corporate assets mainly comprise cash and cash equivalents             公司資產主要包括公司層面所持有之
   held at corporate level.                                               現金及現金等值項目。

40 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                              40 重要會計政策
      (a) Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                           (a) 附 屬 公 司 及 非 控 制 性權
                                                                                   益
            Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The                附屬公司為由本集團控制之實體。
            Group controls an entity when it is exposed, or has rights,           當本集團因參與該實體之營運而
            to variable returns from its involvement with the entity and          獲得或有權享有其可變回報，並
            has the ability to affect those returns through its power over        能夠運用其對該實體之權力影響
            the entity. When assessing whether the Group has power,               上述回報，本集團即對該實體擁
            only substantive rights (held by the Group and other parties)         有控制權。評估本集團是否有權
            are considered.                                                       力 時，僅 考 慮（本 集 團 及 其 他 方
                                                                                  持有的）實質權利。

            Subsidiaries are consolidated into the consolidated financial         於附屬公司的投資自控制權開始
            statements from the date that control commences until the             當日起至控制權終止當日止綜合
            date that control ceases. Intra-group balances, transactions          計入綜合財務報表。集團內部結
            and cash flows and any unrealised profits arising from                餘、交易及現金流量以及集團內
            intra- group transactions are eliminated in full in preparing         部交易所產生之任何未變現溢利
            the consolidated financial statements. Unrealised losses              於編製綜合財務報表時悉數對銷。
            resulting from intra-group transactions are eliminated in the         集團內部交易所產生之未變現虧
            same way as unrealised gains but only to the extent that              損按與未變現收益相同的方式對
            there is no evidence of impairment.                                   銷，但只限於沒有證據顯示減值
                                                                                  的情況。

            Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed                 附屬公司的會計政策已作出必要
            where necessary to ensure consistency with the policies               變動，以確保與本集團採納的政
            adopted by the Group.                                                 策一致。

40 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)                              40 重要會計政策（續）
   (a) Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                           (a) 附 屬 公 司 及 非 控 制性權
       (Continued)                                                              益（續）
       Non-controlling interests are presented in the consolidated             非控制性權益在綜合財務狀況表
       statement of financial position within equity, separately               於權益內與本公司權益股東應佔
       from equity attributable to the equity shareholders of the              權益分開呈列。本集團業績中之
       Company. Non-controlling interests in the results of the                非控制性權益於綜合全面收益表
       Group are presented on the face of the consolidated                     內呈列，作為該年度損益總額及
       statement of comprehensive income as an allocation of                   全面收益總額於本公司非控制性
       the total profit or loss and total comprehensive income for             權益與權益股東之間之分配。
       the year between non-controlling interests and the equity
       shareholders of the Company.

       Changes in the Group’s interests in a subsidiary that                   本集團於附屬公司權益之變動，
       do not result in a loss of control are accounted for as                 如無導致失去控制權，則入賬列
       equity transactions, whereby adjustments are made to                    為股本交易，而綜合權益內的控
       the amounts of controlling and non-controlling interests                股及非控制性權益金額則予以調
       within consolidated equity to reflect the change in relative            整以反映相關權益變動，但並不
       interests, but no adjustments are made to goodwill and no               調整商譽，且不確認任何收益或
       gain or loss is recognised.                                             虧損。

       When the Group loses control of a subsidiary, it is                     當集團失去對附屬公司的控制權
       accounted for as a disposal of the entire interest in that              時，則入賬為出售該附屬公司全
       subsidiary, with a resulting gain or loss being recognised in           部權益，因此而產生的收益或虧
       profit or loss. Any interest retained in that former subsidiary         損於損益確認。失去控制權當日
       at the date when control is lost is recognised at fair value            於該前附屬公司留存的任何權益
       and this amount is regarded as the fair value on initial                按公平值確認且該金額視為初步
       recognition of a financial asset (see Note 40(g)) or, when              確認金融資產（見附註 40(g)）時的
       appropriate, the cost on initial recognition of an investment           公平值或（倘適用）初步確認於聯
       in an associate or a joint venture (see Note 40(b)).                    營公司或合資企業（見附註 40(b)）
                                                                               投資的成本。

       In the Company’s statement of financial position, an                    在本公司之財務狀況表內，於附
       investment in subsidiary is stated at cost less impairment              屬公司之投資以成本減減值虧損
       losses (see Note 40(l)(ii)).                                           （見附註 40(l)(ii)）列賬。

40 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)                                 40 重要會計政策（續）
      (b) Associates and joint ventures                                       (b) 聯營公司及合資企業
            An associate is an entity in which the Group has significant        聯營公司指本集團對其有重大影
            influence, but not control or joint control.                        響力，但無法控制或共同控制之
                                                                                實體。

            A joint venture is an arrangement whereby the Group                 合資企業為一項安排，據此本集
            and other parties contractually agree to share control of           團及其他訂約方同意共同控制該
            the arrangement, and have rights to the net assets of the           安排，及對該安排之淨資產享有
            arrangement.                                                        權利。

            An investment in an associate or a joint venture is                 聯營公司或合資企業之投資乃根
            accounted for in the consolidated financial statements              據權益法在綜合財務報表中列賬。
            using the equity method. Under the equity method, the               根據權益法，投資初始以成本記
            investment is initially recorded at cost, which includes            賬，成 本 包 括 購 買 價、直 接 來 自
            purchase price, other costs directly attributable to the            收購該投資的其他成本，及構成
            acquisition of the investment, and any direct investment into       本集團股本投資一部分的於聯營
            the associate or joint venture that forms part of the Group’s       公司或合資企業的任何直接投資。
            equity investment. Thereafter, the investment is adjusted           其後，該投資經本集團在收購後

            post-tax results of the investees and any impairment losses         之收購後稅後業績及任何減值虧
            for the year are recognised as profit or loss, whereas the          損會確認為損益，而本集團應佔

               Measurement of ECLs                                                         計量預期信貸虧損

               ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit                           預期信貸虧損是信貸虧損
               losses. Credit losses are measured as the present                            的 概 率 加 權 估 計。信 貸 虧
               value of all expected cash shortfalls (i.e. the difference                   損以所有預期現金不足額
               between the cash flows due to the Group in                                  （即本集團根據合約應得的
               accordance with the contract and the cash flows that                         現金流以及本集團預期收
               the Group expects to receive).                                               到的現金流之間的差額）的
                                                                                            現值計量。

               In measuring ECLs, the Group takes into account                             於 計 量 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 時，
               reasonable and supportable information that is                              本集團考慮合理及有理據
               available without undue cost or effort. This includes                       而無需花費過多的成本或
               information about past events, current conditions and                       勞 力 所 獲 得 的 資 料。這 包
               forecasts of future economic conditions.                                    括 過 去 事 件、當 前 狀 況 和
                                                                                           未來經濟狀況預測等資料。

                                                                                                  約事件而導致的虧損。

                  Loss allowances for accounts receivable, lease                              應 收 賬 款、租 賃 應 收 款 項
                  receivables and contract assets, are always measured                        及合約資產的虧損撥備一

                  financial assets are estimated using a provision                            信 貸 虧 損 的 金 額 計 量。該
                  matrix based on the Group’s historical credit loss                          等金融資產的預期信貸虧
                  experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to                       損是利用基於本集團過往
                  the debtors and an assessment of both the current                           信貸虧損經驗的撥備矩陣
                  and forecast general economic conditions at the                             進 行 估 算，並 在 報 告 當 日
                  reporting date.                                                             按債務人的個別因素及對
                                                                                              當前和預測整體經濟狀況
                                                                                              的評估進行調整。

                  For all other financial instruments, the Group                              至 於 所 有 其 他 金 融 工 具，

                  unless there has been a significant increase in credit                      的預期信貸虧損金額確認

               Group considers that a default event occurs when (i)                        行 此 項 重 新 評 估 時，本 集
               the borrower is unlikely to pay its credit obligations                      團認為當下列情況發生時
               to the Group in full, without recourse by the Group                         為違約事件：(i) 借款人不可
               to actions such as realising security (if any is held); or                  能在本集團無追索權（例如：

               considers both quantitative and qualitative information                     下向本集團悉數支付其信
               that is reasonable and supportable, including                               貸 義 務；或 (ii) 該 金 融 資 產

               that is available without undue cost or effort.                             考慮合理及有理據的定量

       Deferred income tax is recognised, in respect of temporary                對於用於財務報告目的的資產和
       differences between the carrying amounts of assets and                    負債帳面金額與用於納稅目的的
       liabilities for financial reporting purposes and the amounts              金額之間的暫時性差異，確認遞
       used for taxation purposes. However, deferred tax liabilities             延 稅 項。然 而，倘 遞 延 稅 項 負 債
       are not recognised if they arise from initial recognition of              產生自初步確認商譽，則不予確
       goodwill. The deferred income tax is not accounted for                    認。倘 初 步 確 認 一 項 交 易（業 務
       if it arises from initial recognition of an asset or liability in         合 併 除 外）中 之 資 產 及 負 債 於 交
       a transaction other than a business combination that at                   易時產生之遞延所得稅既不影響
       the time of the transaction affects neither accounting nor                會計處理亦不影響應課稅溢利或
       taxable profit or loss. Deferred income tax is determined                 虧損，則此遞延所得稅不予入賬。
       using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted or                      遞延所得稅乃採用於財政年度結
       substantively enacted by the statement of financial position              算日前已實行或大致實行並預期
       date and are expected to apply when the related deferred                  於相關遞延所得稅資產變現或遞
       income tax asset is realised or the deferred income tax                   延所得稅負債清償時適用之稅率
       liability is settled.                                                    （及稅法）釐定。

       Deferred income tax assets are recognised only to the                     遞延所得稅資產僅當未來應課稅
       extent that it is probable that future taxable profit will be             溢利很有可能與可動用之暫時差
       available against which the temporary differences can be                  異抵銷時方予確認。
       utilised.

       Deferred income tax liabilities and assets are not recognised             不就於海外業務之投資產生之暫
       for temporary differences arising on investments in foreign               時差異確認遞延所得稅資產及負
       operations where the timing of the reversal of the temporary              債，惟回撥暫時差異之時可由本
       difference is controlled by the Group and it is probable that             集團控制且暫時差異於可預見將
       the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable              來可能不會回撥。
       future.

       Deferred income tax assets and liabilities are offset when                倘有合法可執行權利將即期稅項
       there is a legally enforceable right to offset current tax                資產與即期稅項負債互相抵銷，
       assets against current tax liabilities and when the deferred              及當遞延所得稅資產及負債乃由

       levied by the same taxation authority on either the taxable               或不同應課稅實體徵收之所得稅，
       entity or different taxable entities where there is an intention          且在此情況下擬按淨額基準處理
       to settle the balances on a net basis.                                    結餘時，遞延所得稅資產及負債
                                                                                 方可互相抵銷。

       The Group also incurs various other taxes and levies that                 集團亦產生多種除所得稅以外之
       are not income tax. “Taxes other than income taxes”,                      其 他 稅 項 及 徵 費。「除 所 得 稅 以
       which form part of operating expenses, primarily comprise                 外 之 稅 項」構 成 經 營 支 出 的 一 部

   (s) Provisions                                                                (s) 撥備
         Provisions for environmental restoration, restructuring costs                 如 (i) 本集團因過往事件須承擔現
         and legal claims are recognised when: (i) the Group has a                     時法定或推定責任；(ii) 履行該責
         present legal or constructive obligation as a result of past                  任很有可能導致資源流出；及 (iii)

         required to settle the obligation; and (iii) reliable estimates of            認環境修復、重建成本及法律索
         the amount can be made.                                                       償撥備。

         Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                          如有多項類似的責任，履行該等
         likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement is                  責任導致資源流出的可能性乃透
         determined by considering the class of obligations as a                       過整體考慮某責任類別釐定。即
         whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an                 使同類別責任中任何一項可能導
         outflow with respect to any one item included in the same                     致資源流出之機會不大，但仍會
         class of obligations may be small.                                            確認撥備。

         specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision due                 評估。因時間推移產生之撥備增
         to passage of time is recognised as interest expenses.                        加乃確認為利息支出。

        methods or in which the cost of the required equipment is                       儲量所需的開發設備成本明顯低於鑽
        relatively minor compared to the cost of a new well; and                        探 一 口 新 井 所 需 成 本，可 從 現 有 油 氣
                                                                                        井中進行開採的儲量；及

        operational at the time of the reserves estimate if the extraction is           行 開 採，利 用 儲 量 估 計 時 點 已 安 裝 的
        by means not involving a well.                                                  開採設備和基礎設施可開採的儲量。

Undeveloped oil and gas reserves – Undeveloped oil and gas reserves             未開發油氣儲量 －未開發油氣儲量指預期可
are reserves of any category that are expected to be recovered from             從未鑽區域的新井中採出，或再完井需要較
new wells on undrilled acreage, or from existing wells where a relatively       高支出的現有井中採出的儲量。
major expenditure is required from recompletion.

(i)     Reserves on undrilled acreage shall be limited to those directly        (i)     未鑽區域的儲量僅限於直接扣除開發
        offsetting development spacing areas that are reasonably                        間距區域，有一定把握在鑽後能採油，
        certain of production when drilled, unless evidence using reliable              除非證實運用可靠技術能有一定把握
        technology exists that establishes reasonable certainty of                      在更遠距離在經濟上可生產。
        economic producibility at greater distances.

(ii)    Undrilled locations can be classified as having undeveloped             (ii)    倘已採納的開發方案表明計劃在五年
        reserves only if a development plan has been adopted indicating                 內（在特定環境下須較長時間者除外）
        that they are scheduled to be drilled within five years, unless the             鑽 井，未 鑽 區 域 方 可 分 類 為 擁 有 未 開
        specific circumstances, justify a longer time.                                  發儲量。

(iii)   Under no circumstances shall estimates for undeveloped reserves         (iii)   任何未開發儲量的估計均不得包括擬
        be attributable to any acreage for which an application of fluid                運用流體注入或其他改良採油技術的
        injection or other improved recovery technique is contemplated,                 任 何 區 域，除 非 該 等 技 術 已 通 過 在 相
        unless such techniques have been proved effective by actual                     同 油 藏 或 類 似 油 藏 的 實 際 項 目，或 通
        projects in the same reservoir or an analogous reservoir, or by                 過採用可靠技術證實有一定把握的其

        certainty.
